This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested a multi-step urgent task to switch the recursive generation pipeline from Qwen2.5 to Pythia-1.4B (EleutherAI) on server gpu-6000pro-2. The motivation is that Qwen2.5's pretraining data has too much code, causing LoRA-finetuned unconditional generation to produce code/HTML instead of natural text. Pythia-1.4B (trained on The Pile, text-dominant) is the replacement. The task has 7 explicit steps:
   1. Kill all running Qwen pipeline processes
   2. Clean up invalid outputs (depth_1-3.jsonl, checkpoints)
   3. Download Pythia-1.4B
   4. Verify Pythia-1.4B (model loading, LoRA module names, generation test)
   5. Adapt scripts (lora_finetune.py, run_pipeline.sh, generate_next_depth.py)

   7. Domain consistency check on depth-1 output (first 5 samples, code ratio check)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - LoRA fine-tuning with PEFT on causal language models
   - GPT-NeoX architecture (Pythia) uses fused attention with `query_key_value` module (vs Qwen's separate `q_proj`/`v_proj`)
   - Recursive generation pipeline: train on depth-N → generate depth-N+1 → repeat
   - AutoDL server specifics: network_turbo proxy slows ModelScope CDN (domestic Chinese), must unset proxy for ModelScope downloads
   - ModelScope snapshot_download with `ignore_file_pattern` to skip pytorch_model.bin
   - Domain consistency metric: code character ratio (`{};//<>`) threshold 0.05
   - tmux-based job management via submit_training_job MCP tool
   - conda base environment on aml-train image (not venv)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/lora_finetune.py`
     - Modified to add `--target_modules` CLI argument for flexible LoRA target specification
     - Key change: Added argument parsing and split logic for comma-separated module names
     ```python
     p.add_argument("--target_modules", default="q_proj,v_proj", help="Comma-separated LoRA target module names")
     # ... in main():
     target_modules = [m.strip() for m in args.target_modules.split(",")]
     lora_config = LoraConfig(
         r=16, lora_alpha=32, target_modules=target_modules,
         lora_dropout=0.05, bias="none", task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
     )
     ```
     - Full file was rewritten via `cat > ... << 'PYEOF'` on the server

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/run_pipeline.sh`
     - Modified MODEL_PATH from Qwen to Pythia, added TARGET_MODULES variable
     - Key changes:
     ```bash
     MODEL_PATH="/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b"
     TARGET_MODULES="query_key_value"
     # Each lora_finetune.py call now includes:
     python scripts/lora_finetune.py --input_data data/depth_0.jsonl --output_dir checkpoints/lora_depth_0 --model_name "$MODEL_PATH" --target_modules "$TARGET_MODULES" --gpu 0
     ```
     - Full file was rewritten via `cat > ... << 'SHEOF'`

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/generate_next_depth.py`
     - NOT modified - existing bos_token_id handling works with Pythia

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b` (symlink)

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **SSH combined command exit code -1**: Initial attempt to kill all processes in one command failed. Fix: broke into individual commands.
   - **pkill killing SSH session**: `pkill -f "run_pipeline"` matched the SSH command itself, killing the connection. Fix: used `ps aux` to find specific PIDs, then killed by PID number.
   - **huggingface-cli download failure**: Only printed help text, didn't actually download. Fix: switched to Python `snapshot_download`.
   - **HuggingFace download stalled at 271MB**: AutoDL proxy throttling HF downloads to ~200KB/s. Fix: switched to ModelScope.
   - **ModelScope download slow with proxy (~200-350 KB/s)**: network_turbo proxy was routing domestic CDN traffic through proxy. Fix: `unset http_proxy https_proxy HTTP_PROXY HTTPS_PROXY` before ModelScope download → speed jumped to 17-19 MB/s.
   - **ModelScope path naming**: Saved to `/root/autodl-tmp/models/EleutherAI/pythia-1___4b` (underscores replacing dots). Fix: created symlink `/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b` pointing to actual path.
   - **Downloading both pytorch_model.bin and model.safetensors**: Wasteful. Fix: used `ignore_file_pattern=['pytorch_model.bin']` to skip redundant format.
